@echo off
setlocal enabledelayedexpansion
title Affiliate CRM - apply update
color 0F

echo.
echo  ============================================================
echo    AFFILIATE CRM  -  applying the update
echo  ============================================================
echo.
echo  Nothing to unzip. The new files are inside this file.
echo  It does not matter which folder you run it from.
echo.

set "ME=%~f0"

REM --- find the project folder, wherever this file happens to sit -----
set "PROJ="
call :try "%CD%"
call :try "%CD%\affiliate-crm"
call :try "%~dp0affiliate-crm"
call :try "%USERPROFILE%\Downloads\affiliate-crm"
call :try "%USERPROFILE%\Desktop\affiliate-crm"
call :try "%USERPROFILE%\Documents\affiliate-crm"
call :try "%USERPROFILE%\affiliate-crm"
if not defined PROJ (
  echo  Searching your user folder for the project...
  for /f "delims=" %%D in ('dir /b /s /a:d "%USERPROFILE%\*affiliate-crm*" 2^>nul') do call :try "%%D"
)
if not defined PROJ goto nofolder

cd /d "!PROJ!"
echo  Project folder : !PROJ!
echo.

where tar >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 goto notar
where powershell >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 goto nops

echo  [1/4] Unpacking the new files out of this file...
if exist "crm-update.tgz" del /q "crm-update.tgz"
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -Command "$m='#'+'PAYLOAD'+'#'; $c=Get-Content -LiteralPath '%ME%' -Raw; $k=$c.LastIndexOf($m); if($k -lt 0){ exit 1 }; $b=($c.Substring($k+$m.Length) -replace '\s',''); [IO.File]::WriteAllBytes((Join-Path (Get-Location).Path 'crm-update.tgz'), [Convert]::FromBase64String($b)); exit 0"
if errorlevel 1 goto failunpack
if not exist "crm-update.tgz" goto failunpack
echo         OK
echo.

echo  [2/4] Replacing the old files on disk...
tar -xzf "crm-update.tgz"
if errorlevel 1 goto failtar
del /q "crm-update.tgz"
echo         OK
echo.

echo  [3/4] Checking the files really changed...
findstr /c:"termsAgreedOn" "src\lib\summary-filter.ts" >nul
if errorlevel 1 goto failverify
findstr /c:"HIDE_FROM_SUMMARY" "src\app\(app)\summary\page.tsx" >nul
if errorlevel 1 goto failverify
findstr /c:"listed_unranked" "src\lib\placement-check.ts" >nul
if errorlevel 1 goto failverify
if not exist "src\lib\contact-funnel.ts" goto failverify
echo         OK - this folder now holds the new code.
echo.

echo  [4/4] Publishing. deploy.bat takes over from here.
echo.
echo  When it finishes, press Ctrl+F5 on the Summary page. You are
echo  on the new build when the grey line under the filters reads
echo  "Deals at Terms Agreed are filtered on the day terms were
echo  agreed". CONTACTED will be gone.
echo.
if not exist "deploy.bat" goto nodeploy
call deploy.bat
exit /b %errorlevel%


:try
if defined PROJ goto :eof
if exist "%~1\package.json" if exist "%~1\deploy.bat" set "PROJ=%~1"
goto :eof


:nofolder
echo  STOP - I could not find the affiliate-crm folder anywhere
echo  under your user folder.
echo.
echo  Put this file INSIDE the folder that contains deploy.bat and
echo  package.json, then run it again.
echo.
pause
exit /b 1

:notar
echo  STOP - Windows cannot find "tar". Send me this screen.
echo.
pause
exit /b 1

:nops
echo  STOP - Windows cannot find PowerShell. Send me this screen.
echo.
pause
exit /b 1

:failunpack
echo  STOP - could not unpack the files out of this file. The most
echo  likely cause is that the download was cut short. Download
echo  UPDATE-CRM.bat again and re-run it.
echo.
pause
exit /b 1

:failtar
echo  STOP - could not write the new files. Usually a file is open
echo  somewhere: close any editor or terminal sitting in that
echo  folder, make sure "npm run dev" is not running, try again.
echo.
pause
exit /b 1

:failverify
echo  STOP - the files were written but do not contain the new
echo  code. NOTHING was published. Send me this screen.
echo.
pause
exit /b 1

:nodeploy
echo  The files are updated, but deploy.bat is not in that folder,
echo  so nothing was published. Run your usual deploy.
echo.
pause
exit /b 1

#PAYLOAD#
